Structural cue: “in that” gathers the previous signs; “for one possessing ḥijr” names the receiving faculty. Assigned role: the signs are portions of evidence distributed to an intellect able to restrain misreading. Abductive move: I infer that an oath works here as a divided evidentiary allotment, because the same root also carries apportionment and the later surah tests false allocation of provision. Reading change: the ayah asks whether the preceding images are enough to discipline judgment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B002","root_001226/B003","root_001226/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ab38357b5fc388ca3c10","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:5:4\",\"root_001226\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"89:5:6\",\"root_000296\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yemin, önceki işaretlerin her birine kanıtta ayrı bir pay veren bölümlenmiş bir delil düzenine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B002","root_001226/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_afb2af898607395ec7c6","text":"1. **The oath as an apportioned evidentiary structure.** **Evidence/cue:** `ق س م B004` is oath-taking, while `ق س م B003` is apportionment into shares. The preceding sequence divides time into dawn, ten nights, pair, single, and traveling night; `ح ج ر B002` names reason as restraint. **Mechanism:** apportionment assigns each observed unit a share in the proof, and restrained judgment recombines the shares without erasing their differences. The packet supplies the divisions; I infer their evidentiary cooperation. **Reading change:** *qasam* is heard as both sworn speech and a carefully partitioned argument available to disciplined reason.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c6725449efdc751fb461","text":"The stone-cutting of 89:9 made `ح ج ر B003/B004` unexpectedly concrete; `ذِي حِجْرٍ` became both “possessor of restraint” and a reader positioned before stone-built examples.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000273/B001","root_000296/B001","root_000296/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c981f34547f0bb4ef4d5","text":"2. **Reason as enclosure against transgression.** Lexical evidence: `ح ج ر B001` keeping back by enclosure/prohibition; `ح ج ر B004` enclosed place; `ط غ ي B001` later overstepping; `ج و ب B001` and `ص خ ر B001` later stone-cutting. Structural cue: immediately after this ayah, the surah turns to built, stony, enclosed civilizations. Assigned role: `حجر` is both mental restraint and the stony/enclosing material culture that becomes visible in 89:7-10. Abductive move: I infer a punning material-intellectual bridge: the packet supplies reason, stone, enclosure, and later stone/peg imagery. Reading change: the ayah becomes a question about whether stony power is matched by restraining intelligence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B001","root_001620/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da564b891bee7225beb0","text":"2. **Reason as load-bearing enclosure.** **Evidence/cue:** `ح ج ر B001/B002` joins keeping back by enclosure to the restraining faculty, while 89:7-10 provide pillars (`ع م د B003`), hard rock (`ص خ ر B001`), and driven stakes (`و ت د B001`). **Mechanism:** the material structures supply an analogy for cognition: reason supports judgment by setting a hard boundary against unbounded movement. The packet gives architecture; the transfer from architecture to mental restraint is inferred. **Reading change:** *dhi hijr* is not generic intelligence but a mind able to contain, brace, and stop an impulse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000296/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f4cf85f76f08b702fe0c","text":"89:7-10 supplied a sequence of support, rock, and fixing that made the enclosure inside `ح ج ر B001` materially visible.
